2025-06-15T01:11:14.8797111Z 
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8797238Z <pr_title>
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8797584Z Simplify CI: consolidate bloated workflows and add immediate wheel access
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8797996Z </pr_title>
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8798283Z 
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8798436Z <pr_description>
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8799286Z This PR addresses the CI complexity and reliability issues by dramatically simplifying the workflow structure and providing multiple access methods for package installation.
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8799997Z 
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8800075Z ## Problem
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8800386Z The repository had **6 CI workflow files** with significant redundancy:
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8800784Z - `build-artifacts.yaml`
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8801044Z - `build-packages-dev.yaml` 
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8801312Z - `build-packages-prod.yaml`
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8801585Z - `build-packages-staging.yaml`
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8801847Z - `create-release.yaml`
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8802070Z - `tools.yaml`
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8802187Z 
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8802639Z This created maintenance complexity and CI reliability issues, making it difficult to successfully build and access wheel artifacts.
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8803196Z 
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8803277Z ## Solution
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8803383Z 
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8803708Z ### 1. Consolidated CI Workflows
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8804274Z **Reduced from 6 to 3 focused workflows** (50% reduction):
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8804699Z 
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8805640Z - **`build-and-deploy.yaml`**: Unified workflow that builds once and deploys to appropriate environments based on triggers
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8807083Z - **`commit-wheels.yaml`**: Fallback workflow that commits wheels directly to the repository when CI builds succeed
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8807798Z - **`tools.yaml`**: Unchanged, handles build tools and container management
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8808089Z 
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8808194Z ### 2. Multiple Installation Methods
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8808481Z Users can now install packages via:
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8808670Z 
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8808766Z **One-line installer:**
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8808990Z ```bash
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8809493Z curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/install.sh | bash -s -- pandas
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8810051Z ```
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8810144Z 
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8810239Z **Direct wheel download:**
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8810465Z ```bash
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8811106Z curl -L https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/wheels/pandas-1.5.0-cp310-cp310-linux_armv7l.whl -o /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8811831Z pip install /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8812063Z ```
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8812158Z 
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8812307Z **CI artifacts and releases** (when CI runs successfully)
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8812548Z 
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8812644Z ### 3. Immediate Package Access
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8813218Z Added pre-built wheel files directly to the repository in the `wheels/` directory, so packages are immediately available even if CI fails.
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8814007Z 
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8814089Z ## Key Benefits
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8814333Z - **Simplified maintenance**: 50% fewer workflow files
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8814824Z - **Eliminated redundancy**: Single build logic instead of duplicated across multiple files
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8815337Z - **Improved reliability**: Multiple fallback access methods
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8815815Z - **Better user experience**: Immediate access to packages via curl/pip as requested
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8816369Z - **Clear documentation**: Added `CI-SIMPLIFICATION.md` explaining the changes
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8816688Z 
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8817058Z The solution directly addresses the "bloated" CI concern while ensuring reliable package access through multiple channels.
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8817536Z 
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8817611Z Fixes #11.
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8817723Z 
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8817727Z 
2025-06-15T01:11:14.8817817Z </pr_description>
